		private void SetValueOfNode(string node, int i, object value)
		{

						return;

				{
					return;
				}

			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter(name));
			if (text == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
			if (text == "YES")
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (text == "NO")
			{
				return false;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException();
		}

		private void cmdCancel_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			((MMViewModel)this).CloseForm();
		}

		private string DefaultNewName
		{
			get
			{
				string empty = string.Empty;
				Collection<string> collection = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SG_SPEC", "child_ids"));
				int num = 1;
				while (true)
				{
					empty = $"GRP{num:000}";
					if (collection == null || !collection.Contains(empty))
					{
						break;
					}
					num++;
				}
				return empty;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler varHSDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_varHSDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_varHSDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						if (cmdGenerate_Command != null)
						{
							cmdGenerate_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
						}
					};
				}
				return _varHSDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<int> SelectedRowIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedRowIndexes;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _selectedRowIndexes)
				{
					if (_selectedRowIndexes != null)
					{
						_selectedRowIndexes.CollectionChanged -= _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged;
					}
					_selectedRowIndexes = value;
					if (_selectedRowIndexes != null)
					{
						_selectedRowIndexes.CollectionChanged += _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged;
					}
					((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedRowIndexes");
					OnCanPasteChanged();
					if (_cmdReorder_0_Command != null)
					{
						_cmdReorder_0_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
					}
					if (_cmdReorder_1_Command != null)
					{
						_cmdReorder_1_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
					}
					if (_cmdCopy_Command != null)
					{
						_cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
					}
					if (_cmdEdit_Command != null)
					{
						_cmdEdit_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
					}
					if (_cmdDelete_Command != null)
					{
						_cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
					}
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						UpdateEditDetail();
					}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<int> LowerSelectedRowIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				return _lowerSelectedRowIndexes;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _lowerSelectedRowIndexes)
				{
					_lowerSelectedRowIndexes = value;
					RaiseGenerateCommand();
					((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("LowerSelectedRowIndexes");
				}
			}
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (!CanPaste)
			{
				OnCanPasteChanged();
				return;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			int num = 0;
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				num = SelectedRowIndexes.Count;
			}
			if (num == 1)
			{
				MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "EOConfiguration.SpecGroups", SelectedRowIndexes[0]);
				_isPaste = true;
			}
			else
			{
				MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "EOConfiguration.SpecGroups");
				_isPaste = true;
			}
		}

		private void SetAdvSpecGroupWhenNameEmpty(int selectedRowIndex)
		{
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SG_SPEC", "child_ids"));

					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("groupno", (object)text);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnViewChanged()
		{
			object obj = grdSpecgr_GridData;
			CxvVectorPropertyData val = (CxvVectorPropertyData)((obj is CxvVectorPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			if (val != null && val.Count > 0)
			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int> { 0 };
				}
				else if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 0)
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes.Add(0);
				}
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.Message += Case_Message;
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewChanged();
		}

		public override void OnViewClosed()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.Message -= Case_Message;
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewClosed();
		}

		private void Case_Message(object sender, CxsMessageEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			foreach (CxsMessage message in e.Messages)
			{
				CxsMessage current = message;
				if ((int)((CxsMessage)(ref current)).MessageType != 5)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (UIDispatcher == null)
				{
					OnSimulationRunCompleted();
					continue;
				}
				UIDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					OnSimulationRunCompleted();
				});
			}
		}

		private void OnSimulationRunCompleted()
		{
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMEOSpec_Specgr_Summary)
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("grdSpecgr_GridData");
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			if (pd.Name == "advSpecGroup_var1" && (subPropertyName == "SelectedItem" || subPropertyName == "Items"))
			{
				object obj = advSpecGroup_var1;
				CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData val = (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData)((obj is CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData) ? obj : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					val.DefaultNewName = DefaultNewName;
				}
			}
			else if (pd.Name == "CanGenerate" && subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				if (cmdGenerate_Command != null)
				{
					cmdGenerate_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
				}
			}
			else if (pd.Name == "grdSpecgr_GridData")
			{
				Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					UpdateDetailsVisibility();
				}, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
				if (subPropertyName.Equals("Count", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					if (!_isPaste)
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
						{
							RenameNewRowEmptyID("GRP");
						}, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
					}
					else
					{
						_isPaste = false;
					}
				}
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
		}

		private void RenameNewRowEmptyID(string prefixName)
		{
			string arg = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text = $"{arg}.Input.ENABLED";
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "child_ids", (string)null));
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text2 = array.Last();
			if (text2.StartsWith("#"))
			{
				string text3 = null;
				int num = 0;
				do
				{
					text3 = CreateNewRowId(prefixName, ++num);
				}
				while (array.Contains(text3));
				string text4 = $"{text}.{text2}";
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(text4, "id", (string)null, (object)text3);
			}
		}

		private string CreateNewRowId(string name, int suffix)
		{
			if (name.Length < 8 - suffix.ToString().Length)
			{
				return $"{name}{suffix:000}";
			}
			return $"{name.Substring(0, 8 - suffix.ToString().Length)}{suffix:000}";
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			if (varHSDependencies != null)
			{
				varHSDependencies.Clear();
				varHSDependencies = null;
			}
			if (_lowerSelectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				_lowerSelectedRowIndexes = null;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).Unload();
		}
	}

		{

			if (val.Value != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(val.Value.ToString()))
			{
				openFileDialog.FileName = val.Value.ToString();
			}
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog().Value)
			{
				val.Value = openFileDialog.FileName;
			}
		}

				int num2 = text.IndexOf(".", num + 7);

				{

				}

				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_IsHierarchyBlock", (object)"0");
				}
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		public bool HasResults
		{

				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
				{
					int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + selectedId, "status_code"));
					if (num.HasValue)
					{
						int value = num.Value;
						if ((value & 1) != 0 || (value & 4) != 0 || (value & 8) != 0 || (value & 0x10) != 0 || (value & 0x20) != 0)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		}

		{

			InitializeComponent();
			MMScrollArea1.DataCopying += MMScrollArea_DataCopying;
			MMScrollArea2.DataCopying += MMScrollArea_DataCopying;
		}

		private void MMScrollArea_DataCopying(object sender, MMScrollAreaDataCopyEventArgs e)
		{
			MMScrollAreaMS val = (MMScrollAreaMS)((sender is MMScrollAreaMS) ? sender : null);
			object obj;
			if (val == null)
			{
				obj = null;
			}
			else
			{
				GridControl dataGrid = val.DataGrid;
				obj = ((dataGrid != null) ? dataGrid.DataSource : null);
			}
			DataTable val2 = (DataTable)obj;
			if (val2 == null || val2.Columns.Count <= 0 || val2.Rows.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				MMEOInput_Config_Config.EOClipboardHelper.InitializeSettings(((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel, val2.Columns[0]);
				GridControlClipboardHelper.DoDataCopy(val.DataGrid, ((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel, e.SourceEventArgs, (Func<DataCellInfo, string>)GetCellCxsPath, (Func<DataCellInfo, string>)null);
			}
			finally
			{
				MMEOInput_Config_Config.EOClipboardHelper.ClearSettings();
			}
		}

		private string GetCellCxsPath(DataCellInfo arg)
		{
			if (!((DataCellInfo)(ref arg)).IsEmpty && ((DataCellInfo)(ref arg)).Column.LogicalDisplayIndex == 0)
			{
				IDataCell val = ((DataCellInfo)(ref arg)).Row[MMEOInput_Config_Config.EOClipboardHelper.VariableColumn];
				if (val != null && val.ValueAsString != null)
				{
					return MMEOInput_Config_Config.EOClipboardHelper.CombinePath(MMEOInput_Config_Config.EOClipboardHelper.ItemPathPrefix, val.ValueAsString);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		{
		}

		private string _generateCommandPrompt;

		private ICommand _cmdGenerate_Command;

		private ObservableCollection<int> _selectedRowIndexes;

		private string _VariableCaption;

		public object chkShowDetails_var1 => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(chkShowDetails_var1_Property);

		public string GenerateCommandPrompt
		{
			get
			{
				return _generateCommandPrompt;
			}
			set
			{
				_generateCommandPrompt = value;
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("GenerateCommandPrompt");
			}
		}

		public ICommand cmdGenerate_Command
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdGenerate_Command == null)
				{
					_cmdGenerate_Command = (ICommand)new RelayActionCommand<object>((Action<object>)cmdGenerate_CommandExecute, (Func<object, bool>)cmdGenerate_CommandCanExecute);
				}
				return _cmdGenerate_Command;
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<int> SelectedRowIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedRowIndexes;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _selectedRowIndexes)
				{
					_selectedRowIndexes = value;
					((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedRowIndexes");
				}
			}
		}

		public string VariableCaption
		{
			get
			{
				return _VariableCaption;
			}
			set
			{
				_VariableCaption = value;
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("VariableCaption");
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").ToUpper().Contains("BLOCKS"))
			{
				VariableCaption = Resources.MMEOInput_Config_Config_ViewModel__VariableCaption1;
			}
			else
			{
				VariableCaption = Resources.MMEOInput_Config_Config_ViewModel__VariableCaption2;
			}
			ShowDetails();
		}

		private bool cmdGenerate_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null || SelectedRowIndexes.Count < 1)
			{
				GenerateCommandPrompt = ApwnResources.EOSpecGroup_SelectRow;
				return false;
			}
			string value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.IVNAME.#" + SelectedRowIndexes[0], "value"));
			int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("Top.appModelV8.EO Flowsheet", "has_children"));
			int num2;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && num.HasValue)
			{
				num2 = ((num.Value == 1) ? 1 : 0);
				if (num2 != 0)
				{
					GenerateCommandPrompt = null;
					return (byte)num2 != 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = 0;
			}
			GenerateCommandPrompt = ApwnResources.EOSpecGroup_RunEO;
			return (byte)num2 != 0;
		}

		private void cmdGenerate_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0005: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0039: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Expected O, but got Unknown
			DisplayFormEventArgs e = new DisplayFormEventArgs
			{
				FormProgId = "MMEOInput.MMEOInput_Config_Genera",
				LegacyFlags = 0,
				HelpFile = "eoconf.hlp",
				HelpId = 169,
				ShowPrompts = false,
				ShortName = Resources.MMEOInput_Config_Config_ViewModel__ShortName_GenerateAttributeValuesFormResults
			};
			e.Parameters.Add("OVERWRT", "YES");
			e.Parameters.Add("ATTR", "SELATTR");
			e.Parameters.Add("VALUE", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("PHYSQTY", "YES");
			e.Parameters.Add("UNITS", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("LOWER", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("UPPER", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("STEP", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("TYPE", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("SBWEIGHT", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("SCALE", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("ISMEASURE", "NO");
			e.Parameters.Add("PATHIVNAME", ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.Input.IVNAME"));
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int valueOrDefault = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath("grdVar_GridData.Count", (CxvPropertyWatch)null)).GetValueOrDefault();
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
				{
					if (selectedRowIndex != valueOrDefault - 1)
					{
						list.Add(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath("grdVar_GridData[" + selectedRowIndex + "].MMTextBox1_var1.Value", (CxvPropertyWatch)null)));
					}
				}
			}
			e.Parameters.Add("SelectedVNames", list);
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				e.Parameters.Add("UPDATE", "SELECTED");
			}
			else
			{
				e.Parameters.Add("UPDATE", "ALL");
			}
			((MMViewModel)this).DisplayForm(e);
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).Unload();
		}

		private void ShowDetails()
		{
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMEOInput_Config_Config mMEOInput_Config_Config)
			{
				mMEOInput_Config_Config.UpdateShowDetails();
			}
		}

			private const string EO_AsiPathPrefix = "!EO Flowsheet!";

			private const string CxsPathEOConfigurationPrefix = "Top.appModelV8.EO Configuration.";

			private const string CxsPathBlocksPrefix = "Top.appModelV8.Blocks.";

			private const string CxsPathStreamsPrefix = "Top.appModelV8.Streams.";

			private const string CxsPathCalculatorPrefix = "Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Calculator.";

			private const string CxsPathDesignSpecsPrefix = "Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Design-Spec.";

			private const string CxsPathMeasurementPrefix = "Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Measurement.";

			private const string CxsPathEOInputSuffix = ".EO Configuration.EO Input";

			private const string CxsPathEOSensitivity = ".EO Configuration.EO-Sensitivity";

			public static string ItemPathPrefix { get; private set; }

			public static DataColumn VariableColumn { get; private set; }

			public static void InitializeSettings(CxvViewModel vm, DataColumn variableColumn)
			{
				ItemPathPrefix = "!EO Flowsheet!" + GetTrimmedRecordPath(vm);
				VariableColumn = variableColumn;
			}

			public static void ClearSettings()
			{
				ItemPathPrefix = null;
				VariableColumn = null;
			}

			private static string GetTrimmedRecordPath(CxvViewModel vm)
			{
				return GetCurrentRecordPath((vm.ResolveParameter("main") as string).Replace(".Data.", ".")).Replace(".Blocks.", ".").Replace(".Streams.", ".").Replace(".Flowsheeting Options.", ".")
					.Replace(".Design-Spec.", ".")
					.Replace(".Measurement.", ".")
					.Replace(".Calculator.", ".")
					.Replace(".EO Configuration.", ".");
			}

			private static string GetCurrentRecordPath(string longPath)
			{
				if (longPath.EndsWith(".EO Configuration.EO Input", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					longPath = longPath.Substring(0, longPath.Length - ".EO Configuration.EO Input".Length);
				}
				int num = longPath.IndexOf(".EO Configuration.EO-Sensitivity");
				if (num > 0)
				{
					longPath = longPath.Substring(0, num);
				}
				if (string.Equals(longPath, "Top.appModelV8", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (longPath.StartsWith("Top.appModelV8.Blocks."))
				{
					return longPath.Substring("Top.appModelV8.Blocks.".Length);
				}
				if (longPath.StartsWith("Top.appModelV8.Streams."))
				{
					return longPath.Substring("Top.appModelV8.Streams.".Length);
				}
				if (longPath.StartsWith("Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Calculator."))
				{
					return longPath.Substring("Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Calculator.".Length);
				}
				if (longPath.StartsWith("Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Measurement."))
				{
					return longPath.Substring("Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Measurement.".Length);
				}
				if (longPath.StartsWith("Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Design-Spec."))
				{
					return longPath.Substring("Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Design-Spec.".Length);
				}
				return longPath;
			}

			public static string CombinePath(string path1, string path2)
			{
				if (path1.StartsWith("Top.appModel") || path1.StartsWith("cxs:Top.appModel"))
				{
					path2 = "\"" + path2 + "\"";
				}
				if (path1.EndsWith("!"))
				{
					return path1 + path2;
				}
				if (path1.EndsWith("."))
				{
					return path1 + path2;
				}
				return path1 + "." + path2;
			}
		}

		private void grdVar_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateShowDetails();
		}

		private void GrdVar_DataCopying(object sender, MMScrollAreaDataCopyEventArgs e)
		{
			MMScrollAreaMS obj = grdVar;
			object obj2;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				obj2 = null;
			}
			else
			{
				GridControl dataGrid = obj.DataGrid;
				obj2 = ((dataGrid != null) ? dataGrid.DataSource : null);
			}
			DataTable val = (DataTable)obj2;
			if (val == null || val.Columns.Count <= 0 || val.Rows.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				EOClipboardHelper.InitializeSettings(((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel, val.Columns[0]);
				GridControlClipboardHelper.DoDataCopy(grdVar.DataGrid, ((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel, e.SourceEventArgs, (Func<DataCellInfo, string>)GetCellCxsPath, (Func<DataCellInfo, string>)null);
			}
			finally
			{
				EOClipboardHelper.ClearSettings();
			}
		}

		private string GetCellCxsPath(DataCellInfo arg)
		{
			if (!((DataCellInfo)(ref arg)).IsEmpty)
			{
				IDataCell val = ((DataCellInfo)(ref arg)).Row[EOClipboardHelper.VariableColumn];
				if (val != null && val.ValueAsString != null)
				{
					return EOClipboardHelper.CombinePath(EOClipboardHelper.ItemPathPrefix, val.ValueAsString);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		{
		}

		public void UpdateShowDetails()
		{
			if (((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel != null)
			{
				bool showOrNot = !string.Equals(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel.ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.IDETAILS", "value", (string)null, (CxvPropertyWatch)null)), "NO", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase);
				ShowDetails(showOrNot);
			}
		}

		private void ShowDetails(bool showOrNot)
		{
			if (grdVar == null || grdVar.MMFields == null || ((Collection<MMScrollAreaField>)(object)grdVar.MMFields).Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MMScrollAreaField item in (Collection<MMScrollAreaField>)(object)grdVar.MMFields)
			{
				if (_detailedFieldNames.Contains(item.FieldName))
				{
					((DataColumn)item.DataColumn).Visibility = ((!showOrNot) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
				}
			}
		}

			}
			SetValueOfNode("PLANT", i, value3, overwrite, requireSelected, ParameterToBool("VALUE"));
			SetValueOfNode("PLANT_UOM", i, value4, overwrite, requireSelected, ParameterToBool("UNITS"));
			SetValueOfNode("VALUE", i, value, overwrite, requireSelected, ParameterToBool("VALUE"));
			SetValueOfNode("OFST_UOM", i, value2, overwrite, requireSelected, ParameterToBool("UNITS"));
		}

		private void SetValueOfNode(string node, int i, object value, bool overwrite, bool requireSelected, bool isSelected)
		{
			if ((!overwrite || string.IsNullOrEmpty(GetValueOfNode(node, i))) && (!requireSelected || isSelected))
			{
				SetValueOfNode(node, i, value);
			}
		}

		private void SetValueOfNode(string node, int i, object value)
		{
			if (value != null && value.GetType() == typeof(double))
			{
				double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + node + ".#" + i, "upper_limit"));
				if (num.HasValue)
				{
					if ((value as double?).Value > num.Value)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				else if ((value as double?).Value >= 1E+35)
				{
					return;
				}
				double? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + node + ".#" + i, "lower_limit"));
				if (num2.HasValue)
				{
					if ((value as double?).Value < num2.Value)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				else if ((value as double?).Value < -1E+35)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input." + node + ".#" + i, "value", value);
		}

		private string GetValueOfNode(string node, int i)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + node + ".#" + i, "value"));
		}

		private bool ParameterToBool(string name)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter(name));
			if (text == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
			if (text == "YES")
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (text == "NO")
			{
				return false;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException();
		}

		private void cmdCancel_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			((MMViewModel)this).CloseForm();
		}
